    [UserId] int NOT NULL,

    [Id] int NOT NULL IDENTITY,
    [UserId] int NOT NULL,

    SELECT [o0].[Id], [o0].[OrderId], [o0].[ProductId], [o0].[ProductImage], [o0].[ProductTitle], [o0].[Quantity], [o0].[UnitPrice], [p].[Id] AS [Id0], [p].[Brand], [p].[Category], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[Description], [p].[ImageUrl], [p].[IsActive], [p].[IsFeatured], [p].[OriginalPrice], [p].[Price], [p].[Rating], [p].[ReviewCount], [p].[StockQuantity], [p].[Title], [p].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [OrderItems] AS [o0]
    INNER JOIN [Products] AS [p] ON [o0].[ProductId] = [p].[Id]
) AS [s] ON [o].[Id] = [s].[OrderId]
WHERE [o].[UserId] = @userId
ORDER BY [o].[CreatedAt] DESC, [o].[Id], [s].[Id]
